<tr><td>↳反 divergence</td><td>/daɪˈvɜːrdʒəns/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>分歧，分离，背离</td><td>a sharp divergence of views<br>观点的明显分歧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A wide divergence of opinion delayed the final vote.  意见的巨大分歧推迟了最终投票。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 4

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>gigantic</b></td><td>/dʒaɪˈɡæntɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>巨大的</td><td>a gigantic stone statue<br>巨大的石像</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A gigantic stone statue guards the entrance to the temple.  一座巨大的石像守卫着神庙的入口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 colossal</td><td>/kəˈlɑːsl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>巨大的</td><td>a colossal statue<br>巨大的雕像</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A colossal statue of the founder stands in the square.  广场上矗立着创始人的巨大雕像。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 elephantine</td><td>/ˌelɪˈfæntiːn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>巨大的；笨拙的</td><td>elephantine movements<br>笨重的动作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His elephantine movements on stage made the delicate dance look almost comic.  他在台上笨重的动作让这支细腻的舞蹈显得近乎滑稽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enormous</td><td>/ɪˈnɔːrməs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>巨大的，庞大的</td><td>an enormous amount of data<br>庞大的数据量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The project required an enormous amount of money and patience.  这个项目需要巨额资金和极大耐心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gargantuan</td><td>/ɡɑːrˈɡæntʃuən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>巨大的</td><td>a gargantuan appetite<br>巨大的食量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young athlete has a gargantuan appetite after every practice.  这名年轻运动员每次训练后都有巨大的食量。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 giant</td><td>/ˈdʒaɪənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>巨大的；巨人，巨头</td><td>a giant leap<br>巨大的飞跃</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company took a giant step into the electric car market.  这家公司向电动车市场迈出了巨大一步。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grand</td><td>/ˈɡrænd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>宏大的，壮丽的</td><td>a grand staircase<br>宏伟的楼梯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They held the ceremony in a grand marble hall.  他们在宏伟的大理石厅里举行仪式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 immense</td><td>/ɪˈmens/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>巨大的，极大的</td><td>an immense fortune<br>巨额财富</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The project demanded an immense amount of time and money.  这个项目需要投入巨大的时间和金钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mammoth</td><td>/ˈmæməθ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>巨大的；猛犸象</td><td>a mammoth task<br>艰巨的任务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rebuilding the port after the storm was a mammoth undertaking.  风暴后重建港口是一项巨大的工程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 massive</td><td>/ˈmæsɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>巨大的，厚重的</td><td>a massive iron gate<br>厚重的铁门</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A massive wave struck the harbor wall just after midnight.  午夜刚过，一个巨浪撞上了防波堤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 titanic</td><td>/taɪˈtænɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>巨大的，力量强大的</td><td>a titanic struggle<br>激烈无比的斗争</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two armies fought a titanic battle across the frozen river.  两支军队在冰封的河上展开了一场惊天动地的大战。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tremendous</td><td>/trəˈmendəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极大的</td><td>a tremendous impact<br>极大的影响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new policy had a tremendous effect on small farmers everywhere.  新政策对各地小农户产生了极大的影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vast</td><td>/ˈvæst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>巨大的，广阔的</td><td>a vast desert<br>广阔的沙漠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company owns vast tracts of forest in the north.  这家公司在北方拥有大片森林。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 diminutive</td><td>/dɪˈmɪnjətɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极小的</td><td>a diminutive figure<br>矮小的身影</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She noticed a diminutive figure standing quietly near the entrance.  她注意到一个小小的身影静静立在入口附近。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 infinitesimal</td><td>/ˌɪnfɪnɪˈtesɪməl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>极微小的，无穷小的</td><td>an infinitesimal chance<br>微乎其微的机会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The risk of infection is infinitesimal if the tools are sterile.  只要器械无菌，感染风险微乎其微。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 microscopic</td><td>/ˌmaɪkrəˈskɑːpɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>微小的；显微镜下的</td><td>microscopic dust particles<br>微小的尘埃颗粒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Scientists found microscopic cracks in the metal beam.  科学家在金属梁中发现了极微小的裂缝。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 minuscule</td><td>/ˈmɪnəskjuːl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极小的，微不足道的</td><td>a minuscule amount<br>极小的量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A minuscule crack in the pipe caused the entire basement to flood overnight.  管道上一道极小的裂缝一夜之间让整个地下室被淹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 minute</td><td>/maɪˈnjuːt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极小的，微不足道的；细致的</td><td>a minute quantity<br>极小的量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Even a minute trace of the chemical can spoil the batch.  即使极微量的这种化学品也会毁掉整批产品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pocket</td><td>/ˈpɑːkət/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>袖珍的，小型的；口袋</td><td>a pocket dictionary<br>袖珍词典</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She carried a pocket edition of the poems everywhere.  她随身带着一本袖珍版诗集。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tiny</td><td>/ˈtaɪni/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>极小的，微小的</td><td>a tiny fraction<br>极小的一部分</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only a tiny number of applicants pass the first round.  只有极少数申请者能通过第一轮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>glaze</b></td><td>/ɡleɪz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>给…上釉，妆点</td><td>glaze the pottery<br>给陶器上釉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The artisan will glaze each bowl before firing it a second time.  匠人会在第二次烧制前给每只碗上釉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adorn</td><td>/əˈdɔːrn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>装饰，点缀</td><td>adorn the walls<br>装饰墙面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Old maps and photographs adorn the walls of the study.  旧地图和照片装饰着书房的墙壁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 bedeck</del></td><td>/bɪˈdek/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>装饰，点缀</td><td>bedeck the hall with flowers<br>用花装饰大厅</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Villagers bedeck the hall with flowers before every summer festival.  每年夏季节庆前，村民都用花装饰大厅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decorate</td><td>/ˈdekəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>装饰，布置；授勋</td><td>decorate the room<br>装饰房间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children helped decorate the room with paper lanterns.  孩子们帮忙用纸灯笼装饰房间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 embellish</td><td>/ɪmˈbelɪʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>装饰，修饰；添枝加叶</td><td>embellish a story<br>给故事添枝加叶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He embellished the tale with details that never actually happened.  他给故事添加了从未发生过的细节。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 garnish</td><td>/ˈɡɑːrnɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>装饰，点缀（食物）</td><td>garnish with fresh herbs<br>用新鲜香草点缀</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The chef garnished each plate with parsley and thin lemon slices.  厨师用欧芹和薄柠檬片点缀每个盘子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 strip</td><td>/strɪp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>剥去，剥光（装饰等）</td><td>strip the walls bare<br>把墙剥得光秃</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Workers stripped the walls bare before applying the fresh coat of paint.  工人把墙剥得光秃，然后刷上新漆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 uncover</td><td>/ənˈkʌvər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>揭开，发现，揭露</td><td>uncover the evidence<br>发现证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Inspectors uncovered evidence of fraud in the accounting records.  检查人员在账目中发现了欺诈的证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>glib</b></td><td>/ɡlɪb/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>油腔滑调的；肤浅的</td><td>a glib salesman<br>油腔滑调的销售员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His glib answers convinced no one on the hiring committee that afternoon.  那天下午他油腔滑调的回答没能说服招聘委员会的任何人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nonchalant</td><td>/ˌnɑːnʃəˈlɑːnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>漫不经心的，淡然的</td><td>a nonchalant shrug<br>漫不经心地一耸肩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He answered the accusation with a nonchalant shrug and changed the subject.  他冷淡地一耸肩回应指控，然后换了话题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 oily</td><td>/ˈɔɪli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>油滑的，谄媚的；油质的</td><td>an oily salesman<br>油嘴滑舌的推销员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His oily compliments made everyone in the room uncomfortable.  他油滑的奉承让屋里每个人都不舒服。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cursory</td><td>/ˈkɜːrsəri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>匆忙的，不注意细节的</td><td>a cursory glance<br>匆匆一瞥</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A cursory inspection missed the cracks running along the bridge supports.  一次匆忙的检查漏掉了桥墩上的裂缝。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shallow</td><td>/ˈʃæloʊ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>浅薄的，肤浅的；浅的</td><td>shallow water<br>浅水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His arguments are shallow and easy to refute.  他的论点浅薄，很容易反驳。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 superficial</td><td>/ˌsuːpərˈfɪʃl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>表面的，肤浅的</td><td>a superficial understanding<br>肤浅的理解</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has only a superficial knowledge of the subject.  她对这门学科只有肤浅的了解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 taciturn</td><td>/ˈtæsɪtɜːrn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>沉默寡言的，话少的</td><td>a taciturn farmer<br>沉默寡言的农夫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The taciturn farmer answered our questions with a nod or a shrug.  沉默寡言的农夫只用点头或耸肩回答我们的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abstruse</td><td>/əbˈstruːs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以理解的</td><td>abstruse theories<br>深奥的理论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His lecture was too abstruse for first-year students.  他的讲座对一年级学生来说太晦涩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deep</td><td>/ˈdiːp/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>深刻的，深奥的</td><td>a deep analysis<br>深刻的分析</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The essay offers a deep reading of the novel's ending.  这篇文章对小说结尾作了深刻解读。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 profound</td><td>/proʊˈfaʊnd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>深刻的，深远的</td><td>a profound influence<br>深远的影响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her death had a profound effect on the whole family.  她的死对整个家庭产生了深远的影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>glisten</b></td><td>/ˈɡlɪsn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>闪光</td><td>glisten with dew<br>因露水而闪亮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The wet leaves glistened in the early morning sunlight after the storm.  暴风雨后，湿漉漉的叶子在清晨阳光下闪闪发亮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flash</td><td>/ˈflæʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>闪光；闪耀，闪现</td><td>a flash of lightning<br>一道闪电</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A light flashed briefly in the window across the street.  街对面的窗子里闪了一下光。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 scintillate</del></td><td>/ˈsɪntɪleɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>闪耀</td><td>scintillate with wit<br>闪耀着才智</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her essays scintillate with wit even when the subject seems dull.  即使题目乏味，她的文章也闪耀着才智。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sparkle</td><td>/ˈspɑːrkəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>闪耀，闪亮；生气</td><td>eyes sparkle with joy<br>双眼闪着喜悦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her eyes sparkled when she finally opened the present.  她终于打开礼物时眼睛闪闪发亮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 twinkle</td><td>/ˈtwɪŋkəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>闪烁，闪耀</td><td>stars twinkle overhead<br>星星在头顶闪烁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thousands of stars twinkled above the quiet desert camp.  成千颗星星在寂静的沙漠营地上方闪烁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 winkle</td><td>/ˈwɪŋkəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词、及物动词</td><td>费力弄出，挖出；闪烁</td><td>winkle out the truth<br>把真相挖出来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Reporters slowly winkled out the truth about the secret land deal.  记者们一点点挖出了那笔秘密土地交易的真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dim</td><td>/ˈdɪm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>暗淡的，模糊的</td><td>a dim light<br>昏暗的灯光</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The room was too dim for reading anything but large print.  房间太暗，只能看大字。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>glitch</b></td><td>/ɡlɪtʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>小故障</td><td>a minor glitch<br>小小的故障</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A minor software glitch delayed the launch by nearly two full hours.  一个小小的软件故障使发射延迟了将近两小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bug</td><td>/ˈbʌɡ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>程序错误；小虫</td><td>fix a software bug<br>修复软件缺陷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A tiny bug in the code crashed the entire system.  代码里一个小错误让整个系统崩溃了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 defect</td><td>/ˈdiːfekt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>缺陷，瑕疵；叛逃</td><td>a manufacturing defect<br>制造缺陷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A tiny defect in the wiring caused the entire system failure.  线路上一个微小缺陷导致了整个系统故障。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fault</td><td>/ˈfɔːlt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>缺点，过错；断层</td><td>find fault with<br>挑剔，找毛病</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His only fault is that he never admits being wrong.  他唯一的缺点是从不承认自己错了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lapse</td><td>/læps/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>小过失</td><td>a lapse of memory<br>记忆上的小失误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A brief lapse of memory made him forget his colleague's name entirely.  一时的记忆失误使他完全忘了同事的名字。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 imperfection</td><td>/ˌɪmpərˈfekʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>瑕疵，缺陷</td><td>a minor imperfection<br>细小的瑕疵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A tiny imperfection in the glass ruined the whole lens.  玻璃中一个微小的瑕疵毁了整枚镜片。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 peccadillo</del></td><td>/ˌpekəˈdɪloʊ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>小过失，小毛病</td><td>overlook his peccadilloes<br>忽略他的小过失</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Voters forgave the mayor's peccadilloes because of his real achievements.  因为确有实绩，选民原谅了市长的小过失。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fatal error</td><td>/ˈfeɪtl ˈerər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>致命错误，严重差错</td><td>make a fatal error<br>犯下致命错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ignoring the warning light proved a fatal error for the crew.  忽视警示灯对机组来说是致命错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>gloat</b></td><td>/ɡloʊt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>幸灾乐祸，自鸣得意</td><td>gloat over the victory<br>为胜利自鸣得意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He could not resist gloating over his rival's very public failure.  他忍不住为对手当众失利而幸灾乐祸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crow</td><td>/kroʊ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>得意地夸耀，自鸣得意</td><td>crow about the win<br>为获胜而得意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He crowed about his victory until even his closest friends grew tired.  他为胜利得意个不停，连最亲近的朋友都听厌了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 relish</td><td>/ˈrelɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>享受，喜爱；乐趣，兴味</td><td>with obvious relish<br>带着明显的享受</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He described the meal with obvious relish.  他津津有味地描述那顿饭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 triumph</td><td>/ˈtraɪəmf/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>巨大成功，胜利</td><td>a stunning military triumph<br>惊人的军事胜利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new bridge is a triumph of modern engineering and careful planning.  这座新桥是现代工程与周密规划的巨大成功。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mourn</td><td>/ˈmɔːrn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>哀悼，悲伤</td><td>mourn the dead<br>哀悼死者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole village mourned the fishermen lost at sea.  全村哀悼在海上失踪的渔民。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>gloomy</b></td><td>/ˈɡluːmi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>忧郁的</td><td>a gloomy mood<br>忧郁的心情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She grew gloomy whenever the winter rain lasted more than a week.  每当冬雨连下一周以上，她就变得忧郁起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sullen</td><td>/ˈsʌlən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>闷闷不乐的</td><td>a sullen silence<br>闷闷不乐的沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sullen silence fell over the table when the topic was raised.  提到这个话题时，餐桌上陷入闷闷不乐的沉默。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dejected</td><td>/dɪˈdʒektɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>沮丧的，失望的</td><td>a dejected look<br>沮丧的神情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She left the interview dejected and sure she had failed.  她面试后神情沮丧，确信自己失败了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dour</td><td>/ˈdaʊər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严厉的</td><td>a dour expression<br>严厉的表情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The guard's dour expression discouraged anyone from asking him for directions.  警卫严厉的表情让人不敢向他问路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 melancholy</td><td>/ˈmelənkɑːli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>忧郁，伤感</td><td>a mood of quiet melancholy<br>淡淡的忧郁情绪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Autumn rain always fills him with a vague melancholy.  秋雨总让他心里泛起一种莫名的忧郁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 morose</td><td>/məˈroʊs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>忧郁的</td><td>grow morose and silent<br>变得忧郁沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After losing the election he grew morose and rarely left his apartment.  落选之后他变得忧郁，很少离开公寓。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 saturnine</td><td>/ˈsætərnaɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>忧郁的，阴沉的</td><td>a saturnine temperament<br>阴沉的性情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His saturnine expression discouraged anyone from starting a cheerful conversation.  他阴沉的表情让人不敢开启轻松的话题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 surly</td><td>/ˈsɜːrli/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>脾气不好的</td><td>a surly waiter<br>一个态度恶劣的侍者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A surly waiter took our order without once looking up.  一个态度恶劣的侍者点单时始终没抬头。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 frothy</td><td>/ˈfrɔːθi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（内容等）欢乐轻佻、不严肃的</td><td>a frothy comedy<br>轻松不严肃的喜剧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The theater is showing a frothy comedy about summer romance.  剧院正在上演一部关于夏日恋情的轻松不严肃的喜剧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>gloss</b></td><td>/ɡlɑːs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>简短解释；敷衍潦草做事（甚至忽略）</td><td>gloss over the problem<br>敷衍带过问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editor added a short gloss explaining the unfamiliar legal term.  编者加了一条简短的解释，说明这个陌生的法律术语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abstract</td><td>/æbˈstrækt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>做总结，概括；使分心</td><td>abstract a long report<br>概括一份长报告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She abstracted the key findings into a single page.  她把主要发现概括成一页。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 annotation</td><td>/ˌænəˈteɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>注释，注解</td><td>marginal annotations<br>页边注释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editor added helpful annotations to every difficult passage.  编者为每个难解段落加了有用的注释。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 brief</td><td>/ˈbriːf/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>简短的，简洁的</td><td>a brief summary<br>简短的总结</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave a brief account of the accident to the police.  他向警方简短叙述了事故经过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 epitome</td><td>/ɪˈpɪtəmi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>缩影，典型；摘要</td><td>the epitome of elegance<br>优雅的典范</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That small hotel is the epitome of quiet good taste.  那家小旅馆是安静品味的典范。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 synopsis</td><td>/sɪˈnɑːpsɪs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>摘要，概要</td><td>a brief synopsis<br>简短的概要</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please send a brief synopsis of your novel with the application.  请随申请寄来你小说的简短概要。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disregard</td><td>/ˌdɪsrɪˈɡɑːrd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>漠视，不关注；缺乏兴趣，缺乏关心</td><td>total disregard for safety<br>完全不顾安全</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their total disregard for safety rules eventually caused a serious accident.  他们完全不顾安全规定，最终造成了一起严重事故。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ignore</td><td>/ˌɪɡˈnɔːr/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>忽视，不理</td><td>ignore the warning<br>忽视警告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The captain ignored the warning and sailed into the storm.  船长忽视了警告，驶入了风暴之中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 neglect</td><td>/nəˈɡlekt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>忽视，疏忽；忽略，怠慢</td><td>neglect his duties<br>疏忽职责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He neglected his duties for weeks and was finally dismissed.  他数周疏忽职责，最终被解雇了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overlook</td><td>/ˈoʊvərˌlʊk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>忽视；宽恕；俯视</td><td>overlook a minor error<br>宽容小错</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher chose to overlook the boy's late arrival.  老师决定不追究那男孩的迟到。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 amplification</td><td>/ˌæmpləfəˈkeɪʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>详述，扩充；放大</td><td>need further amplification<br>需要进一步详述</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His answer was far too short and needed considerable amplification.  他的回答太简短，需要大量补充详述。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 scrutinize</td><td>/ˈskruːtənaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>仔细审查；细看</td><td>scrutinize the document<br>仔细审查文件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Auditors will scrutinize every receipt before approving the annual budget report.  审计员会在批准年度预算报告前仔细审查每一张收据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>glossy</b></td><td>/ˈɡlɑːsi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>平滑的，有光泽的</td><td>glossy hair<br>有光泽的头发</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The magazine printed her photograph on thick and glossy paper.  杂志把她的照片印在厚实而有光泽的纸上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lustrous</td><td>/ˈlʌstrəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有光泽的</td><td>lustrous black hair<br>有光泽的黑发</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her lustrous black hair shone under the bright lights of the stage.  她有光泽的黑发在明亮的舞台灯光下闪耀。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 burnished</td><td>/ˈbɜːrnɪʃt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>擦亮的，有光泽的</td><td>burnished copper<br>擦亮的铜器</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The burnished brass handle shone brightly in the afternoon sunlight.  擦亮的铜把手在午后阳光下闪亮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 glistening</td><td>/ˈɡlɪsənɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>闪光的，闪亮的</td><td>glistening wet streets<br>湿漉发亮的街道</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Glistening drops of rain hung from every bare branch.  闪亮的雨珠挂在每一根光枝上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 polished</td><td>/ˈpɑːlɪʃt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有教养的</td><td>polished manners<br>优雅得体的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His polished manners impressed everyone at the formal dinner.  他优雅得体的举止让正式晚宴上的每个人都印象深刻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shining</td><td>/ˈʃaɪnɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>闪亮的，发光的</td><td>shining armor<br>闪亮的铠甲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her shining eyes showed how much the praise meant to her.  她发亮的眼睛显示出这份赞扬对她多重要。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 coarse</td><td>/kɔːrs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗糙的，表面不平整的；粗俗的</td><td>coarse language<br>粗俗的言语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The workers used coarse language that shocked the visiting students.  工人们说的粗话让来访的学生们大吃一惊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>glut</b></td><td>/ɡlʌt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使过量，使充满</td><td>glut the market<br>使市场供过于求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cheap imports have glutted the local market and driven prices down sharply.  廉价进口货使当地市场供过于求，价格大幅下跌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 cloy</del></td><td>/ˈklɔɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使生厌，甜得发腻</td><td>cloy the appetite<br>使胃口发腻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rich dessert began to cloy after only a few bites.  这道浓甜的点心吃几口就开始发腻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cram</td><td>/ˈkræm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>塞满，填塞；死记硬背</td><td>cram the drawer with socks<br>把抽屉塞满袜子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She crammed all her winter clothes into one small suitcase.  她把所有冬衣塞进一个小箱子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fill</td><td>/ˈfɪl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>填满，充满</td><td>fill the glass with water<br>把杯子倒满水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He filled the glass with cold water and drank it quickly.  他倒满一杯冷水，很快喝掉了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 satiate</td><td>/ˈseɪʃieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>（使）饱足（的），过分满足（的）</td><td>satiate his curiosity<br>充分满足他的好奇心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No amount of reading could satiate the curiosity of that boy about stars.  再多的阅读也无法满足那男孩对星星的好奇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 surfeit</td><td>/ˈsɜːrfɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>（使）过量，（使）饮食过度</td><td>a surfeit of information<br>信息过量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A surfeit of information often leaves readers more confused than informed.  信息过量常让读者更困惑而非更明白。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 oversupply</td><td>/ˌoʊvərsəˈplaɪ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>供应过剩；过量供应</td><td>an oversupply of housing<br>住房供应过剩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An oversupply of oil pushed prices down sharply last year.  去年石油供应过剩使价格大幅下跌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sate</td><td>/seɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使饱足，充分满足</td><td>sate one's hunger<br>充分满足饥饿感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A single bowl of thin soup was not enough to sate his hunger.  一碗清汤不足以满足他的饥饿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lack</td><td>/ˈlæk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>缺乏，不足</td><td>a lack of funds<br>资金不足</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The project stalled because of a serious lack of funds.  项目因严重缺乏资金而停滞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 5

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>glutinous</b></td><td>/ˈɡluːtənəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>胶状的，粘的</td><td>glutinous rice<br>糯米</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The glutinous rice stuck to my fingers while I ate.  我吃的时候糯米粘在了手指上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adherent</td><td>/ədˈhɪrənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>追随者，拥护者</td><td>a devoted adherent<br>忠实的拥护者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The movement attracted thousands of adherents in a single year.  这场运动一年内就吸引了数千名拥护者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adhesive</td><td>/ædˈhiːsɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>粘性的；粘合剂</td><td>adhesive tape<br>胶带</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She sealed the heavy box with strong adhesive tape.  她用结实的胶带把重箱子封好。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 cloggy</del></td><td>/ˈklɑɡi/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>粘稠的，易堵塞的</td><td>cloggy mud<br>粘稠的泥浆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cloggy mud clung to the wheels of the heavy truck.  粘稠的泥巴粘在重型卡车的轮子上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gluey</td><td>/ˈɡluːi/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>胶状的，粘的</td><td>a gluey substance<br>胶状的物质</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old pot was coated with a gluey brown substance.  旧锅里裹着一层棕色的胶状物质。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sticky</td><td>/ˈstɪki/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>粘的，有粘性的</td><td>sticky fingers<br>粘乎乎的手指</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children's hands were sticky with melted chocolate candy.  孩子们的手被融化的巧克力糖弄得粘乎乎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tenacious</td><td>/təˈneɪʃəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>黏性强的；不屈不挠的</td><td>a tenacious opponent<br>顽强的对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She proved a tenacious opponent who never conceded a single point.  她是个顽强的对手，从不让出一分。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 viscid</td><td>/ˈvɪsɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有粘性的</td><td>a viscid liquid<br>粘稠的液体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A viscid sap oozed slowly from the cut in the bark.  一种粘性的树液从树皮的切口缓缓渗出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fluid</td><td>/ˈfluːɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>易变的，不固定的；流体</td><td>a fluid situation<br>变动不定的局势</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The schedule remains fluid until the visas finally arrive.  在签证最终到手前，日程仍不固定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>glutton</b></td><td>/ˈɡlʌtn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>贪吃者</td><td>a glutton for punishment<br>自讨苦吃的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only a glutton would order three desserts after such a heavy meal.  只有贪吃的人才会在这样一顿大餐之后再点三份甜点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gorge</td><td>/ɡɔːrdʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>峡谷；狼吞虎咽</td><td>gorge oneself on cake<br>大吃蛋糕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We gorged ourselves on fresh mangoes until nobody could eat another bite.  我们大吃新鲜芒果，直到谁也再吃不下一口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gourmand</td><td>/ˈɡʊrmɑnd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>贪吃的人；美食家</td><td>a hearty gourmand<br>大快朵颐的食客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The gourmand finished three courses before the rest of us started.  这位贪吃者在我们开始前就吃完了三道菜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>goad</b></td><td>/ɡoʊd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>刺激，驱使，激发</td><td>goad him into action<br>激他采取行动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His teammates goaded him into accepting the ridiculous bet.  队友们激他接受了那个荒唐的赌约。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 urge</td><td>/ˈɜːrdʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>催促，力劝；强烈欲望</td><td>urge him to resign<br>力劝他辞职</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her friends urged her to accept the job in Boston.  朋友们力劝她接受波士顿的那份工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abet</td><td>/əˈbet/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>帮助，教唆（做坏事）</td><td>aid and abet<br>帮助并教唆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was charged with aiding and abetting the robbery.  他被控协助并教唆抢劫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exhort</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɔːrt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>敦促，力劝</td><td>exhort them to persevere<br>力劝他们坚持</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The speaker exhorted the students to persevere despite repeated failures.  演讲者勉励学生们即使屡遭失败也要坚持下去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 instigate</td><td>/ˈɪnstɪɡeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>煽动，激起</td><td>instigate a rebellion<br>煽动叛乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several officers were accused of helping to instigate the rebellion in the province.  几名军官被指控参与煽动该省的叛乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prod</td><td>/prɑːd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>促使…行动</td><td>prod her into action<br>促使她行动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her friends had to prod her into applying for the scholarship.  朋友们不得不促使她去申请那份奖学金。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prompt</td><td>/ˈprɑːmpt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>促使，引起；提示</td><td>prompt an inquiry<br>引发一场调查</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The leaked memo prompted an immediate inquiry by regulators.  泄露的备忘录促使监管机构立即展开调查。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 propel</td><td>/prəˈpel/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>推动，驱使</td><td>propel the reform forward<br>推动改革前进</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ambition propelled him to work sixteen hours every day.  雄心驱使他每天工作十六小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spur</td><td>/spɜːr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>刺激物</td><td>a spur to growth<br>增长的刺激因素</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rising exports acted as a powerful spur to industrial growth.  出口增长成为工业发展的强大刺激因素。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stimulate</td><td>/ˈstɪmjəˌleɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>刺激，激发；促进</td><td>stimulate economic growth<br>促进经济增长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Warm rain stimulated the seeds to sprout within a week.  温暖的雨水刺激种子一周内发芽。</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 check</td><td>/tʃek/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使突然停止，阻止；检查，核对</td><td>check the spread of disease<br>阻止疾病蔓延</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Strict quarantine measures checked the spread of the disease.  严格的隔离措施阻止了疾病的蔓延。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 curb</td><td>/ˈkɜːrb/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>控制，约束</td><td>curb government spending<br>控制政府开支</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td><b>gobble</b></td><td>/ˈɡɑːbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>狼吞虎咽</td><td>gobble down the meal<br>狼吞虎咽吃完饭</td></tr>

</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 devour</td><td>/dɪˈvaʊər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>吞食，耗尽</td><td>devour the entire meal<br>吞掉整顿饭</td></tr>

</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gorge</td><td>/ɡɔːrdʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>峡谷；狼吞虎咽</td><td>gorge oneself on cake<br>大吃蛋糕</td></tr>

</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 guzzle</td><td>/ˈɡʌzl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>狂饮</td><td>guzzle a can of beer<br>狂饮一罐啤酒</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quaff</td><td>/kwæf/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>痛饮；大口喝</td><td>quaff a mug of ale<br>大口喝下一杯麦酒</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳近 swill</td><td>/ˈswɪl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>痛饮，大口喝；冲洗</td><td>swill down a beer<br>痛饮一杯啤酒</td></tr>

</tbody>

<tr><td>↳反 nibble</td><td>/ˈnɪbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>小口咬</td><td>nibble at the cheese<br>小口啃干酪</td></tr>

</tbody>

<tr><td><b>goggle</b></td><td>/ˈɡɑːɡl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>凝视</td><td>goggle at the screen<br>凝视屏幕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tourists goggled at the enormous statue for several silent minutes.  游客们默默凝视那尊巨大的雕像好几分钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blink</td><td>/ˈblɪŋk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>眨眼；闪烁</td><td>blink in surprise<br>惊讶地眨眼</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳近 gawk</td><td>/ˈɡɔːk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>呆呆地看，傻看</td><td>gawk at the accident<br>呆看事故现场</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳近 gaze</td><td>/ˈɡeɪz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>凝视，注视</td><td>gaze at the stars<br>凝望星空</td></tr>

</tbody>

<tr><td>↳近 peer</td><td>/pɪr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>同等地位的人，同辈；好奇地凝视</td><td>respected by his peers<br>受同辈尊重</td></tr>

</tbody>

<tr><td>↳反 glance</td><td>/ˈɡlæns/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>扫视，一瞥</td><td>glance at his watch<br>看一眼手表</td></tr>

</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>goldbrick</b></del></td><td>/ˈɡoʊldbrɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>逃避工作和责任</td><td>goldbrick on the job<br>上班偷懒摸鱼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two workers were fired for goldbricking during the busiest week of the year.  两名工人因在全年最忙的一周偷懒躲活而被解雇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 idle</td><td>/ˈaɪdl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>闲置的，未使用的；懒散的</td><td>idle machines<br>闲置的机器</td></tr>

</tbody>

<tr><td>↳近 parry</td><td>/ˈpæri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>躲避，回避（问题）</td><td>parry the question<br>回避这个问题</td></tr>

</tbody>

<tr><td>↳近 shirk</td><td>/ʃɜːrk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>逃避，规避</td><td>shirk responsibility<br>逃避责任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No one on this team is allowed to shirk hard work.  这个团队里没人可以逃避苦活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sidestep</td><td>/ˈsaɪdstep/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>回避，避开（问题）</td><td>sidestep the issue<br>回避这个问题</td></tr>

</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>gorge</b></td><td>/ɡɔːrdʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>峡谷；狼吞虎咽</td><td>gorge oneself on cake<br>大吃蛋糕</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳近 gap</td><td>/ˈɡæp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>缺口，隘口；差距</td><td>a gap in the hills<br>山间的隘口</td></tr>
